    ── Ran iso guide threads
      $ iso guide threads
      Thread commands

      Every command supports --json for machine-readable output.

      Spawning:

        iso thread spawn --project <id> --prompt "..." [options]

          --prompt <prompt>              Initial prompt (required)
          --title <title>                Thread title
          --project <id>                 Project (required)
          --parent-thread <id>           Parent thread
          --parent-self                  Parent to the current thread (ISO_THREAD_ID)
          --provider <id>                Provider override
          --model <model>                Model override
          --reasoning-level <level>      Reasoning level: low, medium, high, xhigh, max (provider-dependent)
          --environment <id-or-path>     Attach to an existing environment (ID or workspace path)
          --new-environment <kind>       Create a new environment (worktree)
          --base-branch <branch>         Base branch for a new managed worktree
          --machine <id-or-name>         Run on a machine (--host is an alias)
          --service-tier <tier>          Service tier: fast, default
          --permission-mode <mode>       Permission mode: accept-edits, auto, or full
          --section <id>                 Create the thread in a section
          --visibility <visibility>      visible or hidden; a child inherits its parent by default
          --file <path>                  Host-readable absolute or uploaded file path
          --image <path>                 Host-readable absolute or uploaded image path
          --origin-kind <kind>           Create a fork thread
          --source-thread <id>           Source thread for a fork
          --source-seq-end <seq>         Last included source event sequence

        Execution defaults resolve from explicit flags, live parent execution, and
        remembered project defaults. With no remembered model, iso uses the explicitly
        requested provider or Codex and resolves its provider-reported default model
        on the target machine. The product reasoning and permission defaults are
        medium and auto.
        accept-edits uses workspace sandboxing with user-reviewed escalation. auto uses
        the same workspace sandbox with provider-native automatic review. full is the
        explicit sandbox and approval bypass. Plan mode is separate from permissions.
        When spawning a subagent, pass --permission-mode full unless the user or task explicitly requests restricted access.
        Parenting is opt-in. Inside a thread, pass --parent-self to parent the new thread to the current thread.
        Hidden threads are for plugin/background workers. They remain addressable by
        ID while staying out of sidebar organization and unread/pending favicon
        attention. Thread lists exclude them unless
        --include-hidden is passed; direct-ID operations remain available.
        A new child thread inherits the visibility of its parent, so the subagents of
        a hidden thread stay hidden too. Pass --visibility to override the inherited
        value. A hidden child still reports its turns and blockers to its parent
        thread; only source-derived forks stay silent.
        A machine selector accepts an exact ID or an unambiguous name. It works with
        an unmanaged --environment path, --new-environment worktree, or the personal
        workspace. It cannot be combined with an existing environment ID because that
        environment already selects its machine. Without the flag, local/primary
        machine resolution is unchanged.

      Forking:

        iso thread fork <source-thread-id> [options]

          --prompt <prompt>              Optional first prompt; omit for an idle fork
          --source-seq-end <seq>         Fork at this source event sequence (tip by default)
          --workspace <mode>             isolated (default) or reuse
          --title <title>                Thread title
          --permission-mode <mode>       Inherit source by default; accepts accept-edits, auto, full
          --visibility <visibility>      visible (default) or hidden
          --agent-context-seed <text>    Persist agent-only context without a first run
          --file <path>                  Host-readable absolute or uploaded file path
          --image <path>                 Host-readable absolute or uploaded image path

        Forks clone the source provider session on the same machine. Isolated forks
        create a fresh managed worktree (or personal workspace for personal threads);
        reuse attaches the source environment. Omit --prompt to create an idle fork.

      Listing:

        iso thread list                           List threads
          --project <id>                         Filter by project
          --parent-thread <id>                   Filter by parent thread
          --archived                             Show only archived threads
          --section <id>                         Filter by section
          --unsectioned                          Show only threads outside sections
          --include-hidden                       Include hidden threads

        iso thread search <query>                 Search threads and messages
        iso thread history <id>                   List prompt history

      Sections:

        iso thread section list
        iso thread section create <name>
        iso thread section rename <id> <name>
        iso thread section delete <id> [--yes]

      Inspecting:

        iso thread show [id]                      Show thread details and pull request status
          --self                                 Target current thread
          --work-status                          Include git working-tree status
          --git-diff                             Include git diff
          --diff-target <type>                   Diff scope: uncommitted, branch_committed, all, commit
          --diff-sha <sha>                       Commit SHA (for --diff-target commit)
          --diff-merge-base <branch>             Override merge-base branch for diff
          --merge-base-branches                  List available merge-base branches

        Shows pull request status for the attached environment branch when available.

        iso thread log [id]                       Show thread event log
          --self                                 Target current thread
          --format <format>                      Output format: json, minimal, verbose
          --limit <count>                        Limit entries
          --after-seq <seq>                      Paginate after sequence number

        iso thread output [id]                    Get the final output of a thread
          --self                                 Target current thread

        iso thread wait <id>                      Wait for a thread status or event (defaults to --status idle)
          --status <status>                      Wait for this status
          --event <type>                         Wait for this event type
          --timeout <seconds>                    Timeout in seconds (default: 1200 / 20 min)
          --poll-interval <ms>                   Polling interval in milliseconds

      Opening threads and files in the app:

        iso thread open <path>                    Open a file in the current Iso thread panel
        iso thread open <thread-id> [path]        Open a thread, optionally with a panel file
          --line <number>                        Line number to focus
          --split <placement>                    right, down, left, top, or replace
        iso thread pane <action> [thread-id]      Maximize, restore, or toggle an open thread pane

        Inside a Iso thread, ISO_THREAD_ID selects the current thread automatically and
        the thread ID argument is omitted for file-only opens. Pass an explicit thread
        ID with --split to open another thread. Outside a Iso thread, pass the thread ID
        as the first argument. A thread already open in a pane is focused instead of
        duplicated. Edge placement creates panes through the eighth pane; at eight
        panes, it replaces the focused pane.
        Pane actions broadcast to connected Iso app windows and affect the matching
        already-open pane without changing its split tree.
        Paths can be thread-relative workspace paths, or absolute paths inside the
        target thread workspace. Absolute paths under ISO_THREAD_STORAGE open as
        thread-storage files for the current thread. Use this for Markdown or HTML
        artifacts you create for the user so they open in the Iso IDE.

      Messaging:

        iso thread tell <id> <message>            Send a follow-up message
          --mode <mode>                          Message mode: steer (default), queue, or auto
          --model <model>                        Model override for this turn
          --reasoning-level <level>              Reasoning level override
          --file <path>                          Host-readable absolute or uploaded file path
          --image <path>                         Host-readable absolute or uploaded image path

        Tell steers by default, delivering the message immediately into the active
        turn. Use --mode queue for non-urgent follow-ups that can wait until the agent
        is free.

        iso thread stop [id]                      Stop an active or provisioning thread
        iso thread retry [id]                     Continue a subscription-limited turn
          --self                                 Target current thread
          --request-id <id>                      Require an exact failed request id
        iso thread cancel-plan [id]               Exit the provider's active Plan mode
        iso thread clear-goal [id]                Clear the provider's active Goal
          --self                                 Target current thread

        `thread retry` is only for a terminal provider subscription-limit failure.
        The server requires accepted input, available execution settings, no newer
        request, and no provider-owned retry. Prior output or tool activity does not
        disqualify the turn. It starts an agent-only system turn containing `Please
        continue.` on the existing provider conversation; it does not resend the
        original prompt or create another user message. When enabled, the Provider
        retry plugin invokes this continuation automatically for timed limits.

      Ownership:

        iso thread update [id]                    Update thread metadata
          --self                                 Target current thread
          --title <title>                        Set title
          --parent-thread <id>                   Assign to a parent thread
          --clear-parent-thread                  Remove parent assignment
          --section <id>                         Move into a section
          --clear-section                        Remove section assignment
          --visibility <visibility>              Set visible or hidden

        iso thread read [id]                      Mark read
        iso thread unread [id]                    Mark unread
        iso thread reorder-pinned <id> [--after <id>] [--before <id>]

      Queued messages:

        iso thread queue list <thread-id>
        iso thread queue create <thread-id> <message>
        iso thread queue update <thread-id> <message-id> <message> [--file <path>] [--image <path>]
        iso thread queue send <thread-id> <message-id> [--mode auto|steer]
        iso thread queue reorder <thread-id> <message-id> [--after <id>] [--before <id>]
        iso thread queue group <thread-id> <boundary-id> --prefix <comma-separated-ids>
        iso thread queue delete <thread-id> <message-id>

      Persisted panel tabs:

        iso thread tabs show <thread-id>
        iso thread tabs set <thread-id> --expected-revision <n> --tabs-json '<json>'

      Lifecycle:

        iso thread archive [id]                   Archive a thread (and children/hidden forks)
          --self                                 Archive current thread

        iso thread unarchive [id]                 Unarchive a thread
          --self                                 Unarchive current thread

        iso thread delete <id>                    Delete permanently
          --yes                                  Skip confirmation

      Read-only commands require a thread ID or --self where supported.
      Mutating thread lifecycle and messaging commands require an explicit ID or --self.

    from pathlib import Path
    import tarfile
    b=Path('/jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/thr_rzreetwcyu/branching-filter-analysis')
    with tarfile.open(b/'coupled-annealing-source-snapshot.tar.gz') as t:

      //! Invariant revisions of earlier flight choices by replaying their random
      //! inputs. Each finite prefix has independent uniform u64 coordinates; an
      //! independent seed supplies its unchanged tail. Symmetric modular updates
      //! preserve this reference law. The MH ratio therefore uses the complete
      //! accumulated likelihood AND every flight proposal p/q correction.
      use crate::{CruiseFilterError, CruiseFilterModel, CruiseParticle, FlightObservation};
      use mh370_particle_filter::{
          Initialization, ParticleModel, Proposal, StratifiedPostResampleMove,
          StratifiedPostResampleMoveContext, StratumId,
      };
      use rand::{Rng, RngCore, SeedableRng};
      use rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng;
      use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
      use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicUsize, Ordering};

      const PREFIX_WORDS: usize = 64;

      #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
      struct RandomTape {
          prefix: Vec<u64>,
          tail_seed: u64,
          noise_seed: u64,
      }
      impl RandomTape {
          fn draw(rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng) -> Self {
              Self {
                  prefix: (0..PREFIX_WORDS).map(|_| rng.next_u64()).collect(),
                  tail_seed: rng.next_u64(),
                  noise_seed: rng.next_u64(),
              }
          }
          fn stream(&self) -> TapeStream<'_> {
              TapeStream {
                  tape: self,
                  position: 0,
                  tail: ChaCha8Rng::seed_from_u64(self.tail_seed),
              }
          }
      }
      struct TapeStream<'a> {
          tape: &'a RandomTape,
          position: usize,
          tail: ChaCha8Rng,
      }
      impl RngCore for TapeStream<'_> {
          fn next_u64(&mut self) -> u64 {
              let result = self
                  .tape
                  .prefix
                  .get(self.position)
                  .copied()
                  .unwrap_or_else(|| self.tail.next_u64());
              self.position += 1;
              result
          }
          fn next_u32(&mut self) -> u32 {
              (self.next_u64() >> 32) as u32
          }
          fn fill_bytes(&mut self, dest: &mut [u8]) {
              for chunk in dest.chunks_mut(8) {
                  chunk.copy_from_slice(&self.next_u64().to_le_bytes()[..chunk.len()]);
              }
          }
          fn try_fill_bytes(&mut self, dest: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(), rand::Error> {
              self.fill_bytes(dest);
              Ok(())
          }
      }

      /// Difference of two identically distributed integers is symmetric, including
      /// after reduction modulo 2^64. No clipping or state-dependent index selection.
      fn modular_step(word: u64, bits: u32, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng) -> u64 {
          let draw = |rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng| {
              let value = rng.next_u64();
              if bits == 64 {
                  value
              } else {
                  value >> (64 - bits)
              }
          };
          word.wrapping_add(draw(rng)).wrapping_sub(draw(rng))
      }

      /// Translation by a signed, shared donor difference. Swapping the ordered
      /// donors reverses both coordinates exactly, including integer division.
      fn donor_translation(
          current: [u64; 2],
          first: [u64; 2],
          second: [u64; 2],
          divisor: i128,
      ) -> [u64; 2] {
          std::array::from_fn(|i| {
              current[i].wrapping_add(((first[i] as i128 - second[i] as i128) / divisor) as u64)
          })
      }

      #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
      pub struct CruiseReplayState {
          pub particle: CruiseParticle,
          tapes: Vec<RandomTape>,
          /// Complete path density relative to the random-input reference measure.
          log_density: f64,
          log_potential: f64,
          prefix_densities: Vec<f64>,
          initial_mach: f64,
          initial_altitude_ft: f64,
      }

      #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
      pub struct CruiseReplayProgress {
          pub iteration: usize,
          pub latitude_deg: f64,
          pub longitude_deg: f64,
          pub initial_mach: f64,
          pub initial_altitude_ft: f64,
          pub initial_mode: mh370_dynamics::LateralMode,
          pub log_path_density: f64,
      }
      impl CruiseReplayState {
          pub fn progress(&self, iteration: usize) -> CruiseReplayProgress {
              CruiseReplayProgress {
                  iteration,
                  latitude_deg: self.particle.flight.aircraft.position.latitude.0,
                  longitude_deg: self.particle.flight.aircraft.position.longitude.0,
                  initial_mach: self.initial_mach,
                  initial_altitude_ft: self.initial_altitude_ft,
                  initial_mode: self.particle.flight.initial_mode,
                  log_path_density: self.log_density,
              }
          }
      }

      pub struct CruiseReplayModel<'a, 'b> {
          pub model: &'a CruiseFilterModel<'b>,
          pub observations: &'a [FlightObservation],
      }
      impl CruiseReplayModel<'_, '_> {
          fn initial(
              &self,
              stratum: StratumId,
              tape: RandomTape,
          ) -> Result<CruiseReplayState, CruiseFilterError> {
              let initial = self.model.initialize_random(stratum, &mut tape.stream())?;
              Ok(CruiseReplayState {
                  initial_mach: initial.state.flight.mach_set,
                  initial_altitude_ft: initial.state.flight.aircraft.altitude.0,
                  particle: initial.state,
                  tapes: vec![tape],
                  log_density: initial.log_prior_over_proposal,
                  log_potential: 0.0,
                  prefix_densities: vec![initial.log_prior_over_proposal],
              })
          }
          fn transition(
              &self,
              mut state: CruiseReplayState,
              observation: &FlightObservation,
              tape: RandomTape,
          ) -> Result<Proposal<CruiseReplayState>, CruiseFilterError> {
              let mut noise = ChaCha8Rng::seed_from_u64(tape.noise_seed);
              let proposal = self.model.propose_random_with_noise(
                  &state.particle,
                  observation,
                  &mut tape.stream(),
                  Some(&mut noise),
              )?;
              state.particle = proposal.state;
              state.log_density += proposal.log_prior_over_proposal;
              state.tapes.push(tape);
              Ok(Proposal {
                  state,
                  log_prior_over_proposal: proposal.log_prior_over_proposal,
              })
          }
          fn replay(
              &self,
              stratum: StratumId,
              tapes: Vec<RandomTape>,
          ) -> Result<CruiseReplayState, CruiseFilterError> {
              let mut iter = tapes.into_iter();
              let first = iter.next().ok_or(CruiseFilterError::InvalidConfiguration)?;
              let mut state = self.initial(stratum, first)?;
              for (observation, tape) in self.observations.iter().zip(iter) {
                  state = self.transition(state, observation, tape)?.state;
                  self.observe(&mut state, observation)?;
              }
              Ok(state)
          }
      }
      impl ParticleModel for CruiseReplayModel<'_, '_> {
          type State = CruiseReplayState;
          type Observation = FlightObservation;
          type Error = CruiseFilterError;
          fn observation_time_s(&self, o: &FlightObservation) -> f64 {
              o.measurement.time.0
          }
          fn stratum(&self, s: &Self::State) -> StratumId {
              s.particle.stratum
          }
          fn initialize(&self, _: usize, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng) -> Result<Self::State, Self::Error> {
              self.initial(StratumId(0), RandomTape::draw(rng))
          }
          fn initialize_in_stratum(
              &self,
              s: StratumId,
              _: usize,
              _: usize,
              _: usize,
              rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng,
          ) -> Result<Initialization<Self::State>, Self::Error> {
              let state = self.initial(s, RandomTape::draw(rng))?;
              let log_prior_over_proposal = state.log_density;
              Ok(Initialization {
                  state,
                  log_prior_over_proposal,
              })
          }
          fn propose(
              &self,
              s: &Self::State,
              o: &FlightObservation,
              _: f64,
              rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng,
          ) -> Result<Proposal<Self::State>, Self::Error> {
              self.transition(s.clone(), o, RandomTape::draw(rng))
          }
          fn log_likelihood(&self, s: &Self::State, o: &FlightObservation) -> Result<f64, Self::Error> {
              self.observe(&mut s.clone(), o)
          }
          fn observe(&self, s: &mut Self::State, o: &FlightObservation) -> Result<f64, Self::Error> {
              let likelihood = self.model.observe(&mut s.particle, o)?;
              s.log_density += likelihood;
              let potential =
                  if likelihood == f64::NEG_INFINITY || o.measurement.time.0 >= self.model.final_time_s {
                      0.0
                  } else {
                      self.model.guide_log_likelihood(&s.particle, o)?
                  };
              let result = likelihood + potential - s.log_potential;
              s.log_potential = potential;
              s.prefix_densities.push(s.log_density + potential);
              Ok(result)
          }
      }

      pub struct CruiseReplayMove {
          pub iterations: usize,
          initial_donors: Vec<Vec<[u64; 2]>>,
          pub donor_proposed: AtomicUsize,
          pub donor_accepted: AtomicUsize,
          pub proposed: AtomicUsize,
          pub accepted: AtomicUsize,
          pub accepted_state_changes: AtomicUsize,
          pub exact_replay_checks: AtomicUsize,
          pub initial_mach_changes: AtomicUsize,
          pub initial_altitude_changes: AtomicUsize,
          pub initial_mode_changes: AtomicUsize,
      }
      impl CruiseReplayMove {
          pub fn new(iterations: usize) -> Self {
              Self {
                  iterations,
                  initial_donors: Vec::new(),
                  donor_proposed: AtomicUsize::new(0),
                  donor_accepted: AtomicUsize::new(0),
                  proposed: AtomicUsize::new(0),
                  accepted: AtomicUsize::new(0),
                  accepted_state_changes: AtomicUsize::new(0),
                  exact_replay_checks: AtomicUsize::new(0),
                  initial_mach_changes: AtomicUsize::new(0),
                  initial_altitude_changes: AtomicUsize::new(0),
                  initial_mode_changes: AtomicUsize::new(0),
              }
          }
      }
      impl StratifiedPostResampleMove<CruiseReplayModel<'_, '_>> for CruiseReplayMove {
          fn apply(
              &self,
              model: &CruiseReplayModel<'_, '_>,
              state: &mut CruiseReplayState,
              context: StratifiedPostResampleMoveContext,
              rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng,
          ) -> Result<(), CruiseFilterError> {
              if context.output_slot == 0 {
                  let replay = model.replay(state.particle.stratum, state.tapes.clone())?;
                  if replay.particle != state.particle
                      || replay.log_density != state.log_density
                      || replay.log_potential != state.log_potential
                  {
                      return Err(CruiseFilterError::InvalidConfiguration);
                  }
                  self.exact_replay_checks.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
              }
              self.revise(model, state, None, rng)
          }
      }
      impl CruiseReplayMove {
          pub fn with_initial_donors(mut self, donors: Vec<Vec<[u64; 2]>>) -> Self {
              self.initial_donors = donors;
              self
          }

          fn propose_revision(
              &self,
              model: &CruiseReplayModel<'_, '_>,
              state: &CruiseReplayState,
              allow_mode_change: bool,
              rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng,
          ) -> Result<(CruiseReplayState, bool), CruiseFilterError> {
              let mut tapes = state.tapes.clone();
              if let Some(bank) = self
                  .initial_donors
                  .get(state.particle.stratum.0 as usize)
                  .filter(|b| b.len() > 1)
              {
                  if rng.gen_bool(0.3) {
                      let a = rng.gen_range(0..bank.len());
                      let mut b = rng.gen_range(0..bank.len() - 1);
                      if b >= a {
                          b += 1;
                      }
                      let pair = donor_translation(
                          [tapes[0].prefix[2], tapes[0].prefix[3]],
                          bank[a],
                          bank[b],
                          [1, 2, 4][rng.gen_range(0..3)],
                      );
                      tapes[0].prefix[2] = pair[0];
                      tapes[0].prefix[3] = pair[1];
                      self.donor_proposed.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                      return Ok((model.replay(state.particle.stratum, tapes)?, true));
                  }
              }
              // Fixed coordinate sets: choice never depends on how many random
              // draws happened to be consumed by either the old or new history.
              let mut stratum = state.particle.stratum;
              let action = rng.gen::<f64>();
              let change_mode = allow_mode_change && action < 0.1;
              if change_mode {
                  stratum = StratumId(rng.gen_range(0..model.model.modes.len()) as u32);
              }
              let mut block = if rng.gen_bool(0.7) {
                  0
              } else {
                  rng.gen_range(0..tapes.len())
              };
              let index = if block == 0 && rng.gen_bool(0.8) {
                  [2, 3][rng.gen_range(0..2)]
              } else {
                  let count = if rng.gen_bool(0.1) {
                      PREFIX_WORDS
                  } else if block == 0 {
                      12
                  } else {
                      16
                  };
                  rng.gen_range(0..count)
              };
              // Fixed symmetric scale mixture avoids mostly sub-resolution updates,
              // especially for the discrete initial altitude.
              let bits = if block == 0 && index == 3 {
                  [60, 62, 64][rng.gen_range(0..3)]
              } else {
                  [56, 58, 60, 62, 64][rng.gen_range(0..5)]
              };
              if !change_mode {
                  if action < 0.2 && tapes.len() > 1 {
                      block = rng.gen_range(1..tapes.len());
                      tapes[block].noise_seed = rng.next_u64();
                  } else if action < 0.22 {
                      tapes[block].tail_seed = rng.next_u64();
                  } else {
                      tapes[block].prefix[index] = modular_step(tapes[block].prefix[index], bits, rng);
                  }
              }
              let proposal = model.replay(stratum, tapes)?;
              Ok((proposal, false))
          }

          /// After global posterior resampling, this kernel may also revise the
          /// initial navigation family. Its declared prior mass enters the MH ratio.
          /// These global moves do not run inside the fixed-stratum particle filter.
          pub fn revise(
              &self,
              model: &CruiseReplayModel<'_, '_>,
              state: &mut CruiseReplayState,
              mode_probabilities: Option<&[f64]>,
              rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng,
          ) -> Result<(), CruiseFilterError> {
              if let Some(p) = mode_probabilities {
                  if p.len() != model.model.modes.len()
                      || p.iter().any(|x| !x.is_finite() || *x < 0.0)
                      || state.log_potential != 0.0
                      || state.particle.flight.aircraft.time.0 != model.model.final_time_s
                  {
                      return Err(CruiseFilterError::InvalidConfiguration);
                  }
              }
              for _ in 0..self.iterations {
                  let (proposal, donor) =
                      self.propose_revision(model, state, mode_probabilities.is_some(), rng)?;
                  let stratum = proposal.particle.stratum;
                  let log_mode_ratio = mode_probabilities
                      .map(|p| p[stratum.0 as usize].ln() - p[state.particle.stratum.0 as usize].ln())
                      .unwrap_or(0.0);
                  self.proposed.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                  let ratio = proposal.log_density + proposal.log_potential
                      - state.log_density
                      - state.log_potential
                      + log_mode_ratio;
                  if ratio.is_nan() || ratio == f64::INFINITY {
                      return Err(CruiseFilterError::InvalidConfiguration);
                  }
                  if rng.gen::<f64>().ln() < ratio.min(0.0) {
                      self.accepted.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                      if donor {
                          self.donor_accepted.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                      }
                      if proposal.particle.flight.aircraft != state.particle.flight.aircraft {
                          self.accepted_state_changes.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                      }
                      if proposal.initial_mach != state.initial_mach {
                          self.initial_mach_changes.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                      }
                      if proposal.initial_altitude_ft != state.initial_altitude_ft {
                          self.initial_altitude_changes
                              .fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                      }
                      if proposal.particle.stratum != state.particle.stratum {
                          self.initial_mode_changes.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
                      }
                      *state = proposal;
                  }
              }
              Ok(())
          }
      }

      # Particle Gibbs with ancestor sampling: independent finite reference

      Lindsten, F., Jordan, M. I., and Schön, T. B. (2014), “Particle Gibbs with
      Ancestor Sampling”, *Journal of Machine Learning Research* 15, 2145–2184.
      Published paper: https://jmlr.org/papers/volume15/lindsten14a/lindsten14a.pdf
      Local PDF SHA256: `0e6852b82dfa02c1e5164c0c27d7734942e10d11a3e394c616e13052e55b0935`.

      This independently checks the invariant-target claim of Algorithm 2, not the
      paper's numerical figures or an MH370 result. The fixture has three binary
      innovations, an unequal proposal, a history-dependent physical prior and a
      final likelihood that depends on the initial innovation. All probabilities
      and every draw of a two-particle sweep are enumerated as exact rational numbers.
      No random seed, units, coordinates, external data or floating-point tolerance
      is needed for the invariant check. Decimal matrices are supplied for a separate
      implementation comparison. Normalization is exact before decimal export.

      Reproduce with:

      ```sh
      python3 .sources/lindsten-2014-particle-gibbs-ancestor-sampling/code/finite_reference.py
      ```

      The full ancestor weight uses the entire retained suffix, as in equation (3),
      pp.2149–2150, and equation (22), p.2164. The deliberately truncated control
      omits the distant dependence and is required to fail. Ordinary particle Gibbs
      without ancestor sampling is also required to preserve the target. Correct
      invariance is necessary but does not establish fast exploration or convergence
      for aircraft trajectories.

      Citation ledger:

      | Claim | Locator | Located passage / equation | Status |
      |---|---|---|---|
      | Ancestor sampling weights combine the filtering mass and full suffix target ratio. | §3.2, p.2150, eq.(3), Algorithm 2 | Equation (3): `w_i * gamma_T(prefix_i,suffix_ref)/gamma_(t-1)(prefix_i)` | FOUND |
      | Simulator-driving innovations can support ancestor sampling when physical transition density is unavailable. | §6.2, pp.2163–2164 | “by expressing an SSM in terms of its ‘innovations’” followed by the unavailable-transition-density construction | FOUND |
      | A history-dependent model requires the complete later product. | §6.2, p.2164, eq.(22) | Product from `s=t` through `T`; truncation is explicitly introduced as an approximation in eq.(23). | FOUND |

      The proposed MH370 application is our inference from these equations. It
      retains the current scientific model and evaluates the exact suffix, including
      changes to marginalized frequency bias and fuel. It must be tested separately.
    ── Created .sources/lindsten-2014-particle-gibbs-ancestor-sampling/code/finite_reference.py +94
      """Exact finite-state recreation of PGAS Algorithm 2, without product imports.

      Three binary innovations have unequal independent proposal probabilities.
      The physical prior and final observation depend on earlier innovations.
      Enumerate every draw in a two-particle sweep using rational arithmetic.
      """
      from fractions import Fraction as F
      from itertools import product
      from pathlib import Path
      import json

      PATHS = list(product(range(2), repeat=3))
      Q = [F(3, 4), F(1, 3), F(3, 5)]


      def prior_one(prefix, step):
          if step == 0:
              return F(1, 4)
          if step == 1:
              return F(4, 5) if prefix[0] else F(2, 5)
          return F(3, 4) if prefix[0] != prefix[1] else F(1, 5)


      def likelihood(path, step):
          if step == 0:
              return F(7, 10) if path[0] else F(3, 10)
          if step == 1:
              return F(4, 5) if path[0] == path[1] else F(1, 10)
          return F(9, 10) if path[2] == path[0] else F(1, 20)


      def incremental_weight(path, step):
          p, q = prior_one(path[:step], step), Q[step]
          if not path[step]:
              p, q = 1 - p, 1 - q
          return p / q * likelihood(path, step)


      def probabilities(weights):
          total = sum(weights)
          return [x / total for x in weights]


      def exact_target():
          masses = []
          for path in PATHS:
              mass = F(1)
              for step, bit in enumerate(path):
                  p = prior_one(path[:step], step)
                  mass *= (p if bit else 1 - p) * likelihood(path, step)
              masses.append(mass)
          return probabilities(masses), sum(masses)


      def transition(reference, algorithm):
          result = [F(0) for _ in PATHS]

          def propagate(paths, step, probability):
              weights = [incremental_weight(p, step - 1) for p in paths]
              parent = probabilities(weights)
              if step == 3:
                  for path, mass in zip(paths, parent):
                      result[PATHS.index(tuple(path))] += probability * mass
                  return
              if algorithm == "pg":
                  ancestor = [F(0), F(1)]
              else:
                  ancestor = []
                  for p, w in zip(paths, weights):
                      joined = p + reference[step:]
                      # Omitting distant factors is deliberately wrong for this
                      # non-Markovian example and must fail the invariant check.
                      end = step + 1 if algorithm == "truncated" else 3
                      for j in range(step, end):
                          w *= incremental_weight(joined, j)
                      ancestor.append(w)
                  ancestor = probabilities(ancestor)
              for a, bit, ar in product(range(2), repeat=3):
                  mass = probability * parent[a] * (Q[step] if bit else 1 - Q[step]) * ancestor[ar]
                  if mass:
                      propagate([paths[a] + (bit,), paths[ar] + (reference[step],)], step + 1, mass)

          for bit in range(2):
              propagate([(bit,), (reference[0],)], 1, Q[0] if bit else 1 - Q[0])
          assert sum(result) == 1
          return result


      def main():
          target, evidence = exact_target()
          result = {"physical_evidence": str(evidence), "paths": PATHS,
                    "target_probabilities": [str(x) for x in target], "algorithms": {}}
          for method in ["pgas", "pg", "truncated"]:
              matrix = [transition(p, method) for p in PATHS]
              after = [sum(target[i] * matrix[i][j] for i in range(8)) for j in range(8)]
              error = max(abs(a - b) for a, b in zip(after, target))
              result["algorithms"][method] = {
                  "maximum_invariance_error": float(error),
                  "exact_invariance": error == 0,
                  "stationary_probability_initial_bit_changes": float(sum(
                      target[i] * matrix[i][j] for i in range(8) for j in range(8)
                      if PATHS[i][0] != PATHS[j][0])),
                  "transition_matrix": [[float(x) for x in row] for row in matrix],
              }
              assert (error == 0) == (method != "truncated")
          output = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "outputs" / "finite-reference.json"
          output.write_text(json.dumps(result, indent=2) + "\n")
          print(json.dumps({"physical_evidence": str(evidence), "algorithms": {
              k: {a: b for a, b in v.items() if a != "transition_matrix"}
              for k, v in result["algorithms"].items()}}))


      if __name__ == "__main__":
          main()

    ── Created /tmp/mh370-cruise-ancestor-sampling/crates/particle-filter/src/ancestor_sampling.rs +227
      //! Conditional SMC on simulator innovations, with exact full-suffix ancestor
      //! weights. Lindsten, Jordan & Schön (2014), Algorithm 2 and equations 3/22.
      //! This kernel returns a correlated path update, not an evidence estimate.
      use crate::{normalize_log_weights, rng_for, SmcError};
      use rand::Rng;
      use rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng;
      use rayon::prelude::*;
      use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};

      /// The reference measure for each innovation must be independent of the
      /// earlier path. `extend` returns the target increment relative to that measure,
      /// including all physical-prior/proposal corrections and actual likelihoods.
      pub trait InnovationHistoryModel: Sync {
          type State: Clone + Send + Sync;
          type Innovation: Clone + Send + Sync;
          type Error: std::error::Error + Send + Sync + 'static;
          fn steps(&self) -> usize;
          fn draw(&self, step: usize, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng) -> Self::Innovation;
          fn extend(
              &self,
              previous: Option<&Self::State>,
              innovation: &Self::Innovation,
              step: usize,
          ) -> Result<(Self::State, f64), Self::Error>;
      }

      #[derive(Clone)]
      pub struct InnovationPath<S, I> {
          pub state: S,
          pub innovations: Vec<I>,
          pub log_increments: Vec<f64>,
      }

      #[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]
      pub struct AncestorSamplingStep {
          pub step: usize,
          pub reference_parent: Option<usize>,
          pub ancestor_effective_samples: Option<f64>,
          pub maximum_ancestor_probability: Option<f64>,
          pub filtering_effective_samples: f64,
          pub suffix_evaluations: usize,
      }

      pub struct AncestorSamplingResult<S, I> {
          pub path: InnovationPath<S, I>,
          pub diagnostics: Vec<AncestorSamplingStep>,
      }

      fn valid(x: f64) -> Result<f64, SmcError> {
          if x.is_finite() || x == f64::NEG_INFINITY {
              Ok(x)
          } else {
              Err(SmcError::Model("invalid innovation target increment".into()))
          }
      }

      fn probabilities(log: &[f64]) -> Result<Vec<f64>, SmcError> {
          Ok(normalize_log_weights(log)?.0.iter().map(|x| x.exp()).collect())
      }

      fn select(probabilities: &[f64], rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng) -> usize {
          let u = rng.gen::<f64>();
          let mut cumulative = 0.0;
          for (i, p) in probabilities.iter().enumerate() {
              cumulative += p;
              if u < cumulative {
                  return i;
              }
          }
          // Absorb only floating-point summation error in the last positive cell.
          probabilities.iter().rposition(|p| *p > 0.0).unwrap()
      }

      fn append<M: InnovationHistoryModel>(
          model: &M,
          previous: Option<&InnovationPath<M::State, M::Innovation>>,
          innovation: M::Innovation,
          step: usize,
      ) -> Result<InnovationPath<M::State, M::Innovation>, SmcError> {
          let (state, increment) = model
              .extend(previous.map(|p| &p.state), &innovation, step)
              .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;
          let mut innovations = previous.map_or_else(Vec::new, |p| p.innovations.clone());
          let mut log_increments = previous.map_or_else(Vec::new, |p| p.log_increments.clone());
          innovations.push(innovation);
          log_increments.push(valid(increment)?);
          Ok(InnovationPath { state, innovations, log_increments })
      }

      /// `None` initializes a reference with an ordinary multinomial-resampling PF.
      /// Later calls condition on the supplied path. All reference suffix factors
      /// are evaluated; no Markov approximation or truncated lookahead is used.
      pub fn sample_innovation_history<M: InnovationHistoryModel>(
          model: &M,
          reference: Option<&[M::Innovation]>,
          particles: usize,
          seed: u64,
          iteration: usize,
          ancestor_sampling: bool,
      ) -> Result<AncestorSamplingResult<M::State, M::Innovation>, SmcError> {
          if particles == 0 || model.steps() == 0
              || reference.is_some_and(|r| r.len() != model.steps())
          {
              return Err(SmcError::Model("invalid conditional history dimensions".into()));
          }
          let mut paths: Vec<InnovationPath<M::State, M::Innovation>> = Vec::new();
          let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
          for step in 0..model.steps() {
              let previous_probabilities = if step == 0 { Vec::new() } else {
                  probabilities(&paths.iter().map(|p| *p.log_increments.last().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>())?
              };
              let mut reference_parent = reference.map(|_| particles - 1);
              let mut ancestor_effective_samples = None;
              let mut maximum_ancestor_probability = None;
              let mut suffix_evaluations = 0;
              if step > 0 && ancestor_sampling {
                  if let Some(retained) = reference {
                      let suffix: Vec<Result<f64, SmcError>> = paths.par_iter().enumerate().map(|(i, p)| {
                          if previous_probabilities[i] == 0.0 {
                              return Ok(f64::NEG_INFINITY);
                          }
                          let mut state = p.state.clone();
                          let mut log_ratio = 0.0;
                          for future in step..model.steps() {
                              let (next, factor) = model.extend(Some(&state), &retained[future], future)
                                  .map_err(|e| SmcError::Model(e.to_string()))?;
                              log_ratio += valid(factor)?;
                              state = next;
                              if log_ratio == f64::NEG_INFINITY { break; }
                          }
                          Ok(previous_probabilities[i].ln() + log_ratio)
                      }).collect();
                      let a = probabilities(&suffix.into_iter().collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?)?;
                      let mut rng = rng_for(seed, "innovation-history-ancestor", iteration, step, 0);
                      reference_parent = Some(select(&a, &mut rng));
                      ancestor_effective_samples = Some(1.0 / a.iter().map(|x| x*x).sum::<f64>());
                      maximum_ancestor_probability = Some(a.iter().copied().fold(0.0, f64::max));
                      suffix_evaluations = paths.len();
                  }
              }
              let next: Vec<Result<_, SmcError>> = (0..particles).into_par_iter().map(|i| {
                  let retained = reference.filter(|_| i + 1 == particles);
                  let mut rng = rng_for(seed, "innovation-history-forward", iteration, step, i);
                  let parent = if step == 0 { None } else if retained.is_some() {
                      Some(&paths[reference_parent.unwrap()])
                  } else {
                      Some(&paths[select(&previous_probabilities, &mut rng)])
                  };
                  let innovation = retained.map_or_else(|| model.draw(step, &mut rng), |r| r[step].clone());
                  append(model, parent, innovation, step)
              }).collect();
              paths = next.into_iter().collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?;
              let p = probabilities(&paths.iter().map(|p| *p.log_increments.last().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>())?;
              diagnostics.push(AncestorSamplingStep {
                  step, reference_parent, ancestor_effective_samples, maximum_ancestor_probability,
                  filtering_effective_samples: 1.0 / p.iter().map(|x| x*x).sum::<f64>(),
                  suffix_evaluations,
              });
          }
          let p = probabilities(&paths.iter().map(|p| *p.log_increments.last().unwrap()).collect::<Vec<_>>())?;
          let mut rng = rng_for(seed, "innovation-history-output", iteration, 0, 0);
          let index = select(&p, &mut rng);
          Ok(AncestorSamplingResult { path: paths.swap_remove(index), diagnostics })
      }

      #[cfg(test)]
      mod tests {
          use super::*;
          use std::convert::Infallible;

          struct Binary;
          impl InnovationHistoryModel for Binary {
              type State = Vec<u8>;
              type Innovation = u8;
              type Error = Infallible;
              fn steps(&self) -> usize { 3 }
              fn draw(&self, t: usize, rng: &mut ChaCha8Rng) -> u8 {
                  u8::from(rng.gen_bool([0.75, 1.0/3.0, 0.6][t]))
              }
              fn extend(&self, previous: Option<&Vec<u8>>, bit: &u8, t: usize) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, f64), Infallible> {
                  let mut p = previous.cloned().unwrap_or_default();
                  let prior = match t {
                      0 => 0.25,
                      1 => if p[0] == 1 { 0.8 } else { 0.4 },
                      _ => if p[0] != p[1] { 0.75 } else { 0.2 },
                  };
                  let proposal = [0.75, 1.0/3.0, 0.6][t];
                  p.push(*bit);
                  let likelihood = match t {
                      0 => if *bit == 1 { 0.7 } else { 0.3 },
                      1 => if p[0] == p[1] { 0.8 } else { 0.1 },
                      _ => if p[0] == p[2] { 0.9 } else { 0.05 },
                  };
                  let ratio = if *bit == 1 { prior/proposal } else { (1.0-prior)/(1.0-proposal) };
                  Ok((p, (ratio * likelihood).ln()))
              }
          }

          #[test]
          fn one_particle_retains_exact_reference() {
              for mask in 0..8 {
                  let r: Vec<u8> = (0..3).map(|i| ((mask >> (2-i)) & 1) as u8).collect();
                  let result = sample_innovation_history(&Binary, Some(&r), 1, 17, 0, true).unwrap();
                  assert_eq!(result.path.state, r);
              }
          }

          #[test]
          fn complete_suffix_kernel_matches_independently_enumerated_transition_rows() {
              // Independently enumerated rational transition matrix from the paper
              // recreation, copied as numeric fixtures (no product->sources import).
              let expected: Vec<Vec<f64>> = serde_json::from_str(include_str!("ancestor_sampling_fixture.json")).unwrap();
              let repetitions = 12000;
              let pool = rayon::ThreadPoolBuilder::new().num_threads(1).build().unwrap();
              pool.install(|| for mask in 0..8 {
                  let r: Vec<u8> = (0..3).map(|i| ((mask >> (2-i)) & 1) as u8).collect();
                  let mut counts = [0usize;8];
                  for n in 0..repetitions {
                      let result = sample_innovation_history(&Binary, Some(&r), 2, 817100 + mask, n, true).unwrap();
                      let j = result.path.state.iter().fold(0, |a, x| 2*a + *x as usize);
                      counts[j] += 1;
                  }
                  for j in 0..8 {
                      let p = expected[mask as usize][j];
                      let sd = (p*(1.0-p)/repetitions as f64).sqrt();
                      assert!((counts[j] as f64/repetitions as f64-p).abs() < 7.0*sd + 1.0/repetitions as f64);
                  }
              });
          }

          #[test]
          fn history_kernel_is_reproducible_across_worker_counts() {
              let reference = [0,1,1];
              let run = |threads| rayon::ThreadPoolBuilder::new().num_threads(threads).build().unwrap().install(||
                  sample_innovation_history(&Binary, Some(&reference), 24, 1932, 7, true).unwrap());
              let a = run(1); let b = run(4);
              assert_eq!(a.path.state, b.path.state);
              assert_eq!(a.path.log_increments, b.path.log_increments);
              assert_eq!(serde_json::to_string(&a.diagnostics).unwrap(), serde_json::to_string(&b.diagnostics).unwrap());
          }
      }

      //! Focused innovation-space particle Gibbs control on an existing synthetic
      //! flight. Complete original observations configure terminal fuel conditioning.
      use anyhow::{bail, Context, Result};
      use clap::Parser;
      use mh370_dynamics::{CruiseEnvironment, Era5Grid, IgrfGrid};
      use mh370_estimator::{parse_satcom_observations, CruiseFilterConfig, CruiseFilterError, CruiseFilterModel, CruiseParticle, FlightObservation};
      use mh370_particle_filter::{derive_seed, rng_for, run_stratified_filter_with_snapshot_retention, sample_innovation_history, FilterCheckpoint, FilterConfig, InnovationHistoryModel, ObservationBlockModel, ParticleModel, SnapshotRetention, StratifiedFilterPlan, StratumId};
      use rand::{Rng, RngCore, SeedableRng};
      use rand_chacha::ChaCha8Rng;
      use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
      use serde_json::{json, Value};
      use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};

      #[derive(Parser)]
      struct Arguments {
          #[arg(long)] source: PathBuf,
          #[arg(long)] output: PathBuf,
          #[arg(long,default_value_t=4)] through_block: usize,
          #[arg(long,default_value_t=4)] threads: usize,
          #[arg(long)] reference: Option<PathBuf>,
          #[arg(long,default_value_t=64)] particles: usize,
          #[arg(long,default_value_t=32)] iterations: usize,
          #[arg(long,default_value_t=37118101)] seed: u64,
          #[arg(long)] without_ancestor_sampling: bool,
      }

      #[derive(Clone,Debug,PartialEq,Serialize,Deserialize)]
      struct Innovation { seed: [u8;32], initial_mode: Option<u32> }

      struct CruiseHistory<'a,'b> {
          model: &'a CruiseFilterModel<'b>,
          blocks: &'a [Vec<FlightObservation>],
          mode_probabilities: Vec<f64>,
      }

      impl InnovationHistoryModel for CruiseHistory<'_, '_> {
          type State=CruiseParticle;
          type Innovation=Innovation;
          type Error=CruiseFilterError;
          fn steps(&self)->usize { 1+self.blocks.len() }
          fn draw(&self, step:usize, rng:&mut ChaCha8Rng)->Innovation {
              let initial_mode = if step==0 {
                  let u=rng.gen::<f64>(); let mut sum=0.0;
                  Some(self.mode_probabilities.iter().position(|p| {sum+=p;u<sum}).unwrap_or(self.mode_probabilities.len()-1) as u32)
              } else {None};
              let mut seed=[0;32];rng.fill_bytes(&mut seed);
              Innovation{seed,initial_mode}
          }
          fn extend(&self, previous:Option<&CruiseParticle>, input:&Innovation, step:usize)->Result<(CruiseParticle,f64),CruiseFilterError> {
              let mut rng=ChaCha8Rng::from_seed(input.seed);
              if step==0 {
                  let initial=self.model.initialize_in_stratum(StratumId(input.initial_mode.ok_or(CruiseFilterError::InvalidConfiguration)?),0,0,1,&mut rng)?;
                  return Ok((initial.state,initial.log_prior_over_proposal));
              }
              let mut state=previous.ok_or(CruiseFilterError::InvalidConfiguration)?.clone();
              let mut increment=0.0;
              for o in &self.blocks[step-1] {
                  let elapsed=o.measurement.time.0-state.flight.aircraft.time.0;
                  let p=self.model.propose(&state,o,elapsed,&mut rng)?;
                  state=p.state;
                  increment+=p.log_prior_over_proposal+self.model.observe(&mut state,o)?;
              }
              Ok((state,increment))
          }
      }

      fn read(p:&Path)->Result<Value> {Ok(serde_json::from_slice(&fs::read(p)?)?)}
      fn write(p:&Path,v:&Value)->Result<()> {fs::write(p,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(v)?)?;Ok(())}
      fn sha(b:&[u8])->String {hex::encode(Sha256::digest(b))}
      fn close(a:f64,b:f64)->Result<()> {if (a-b).abs()>2e-9*b.abs().max(1.0) {bail!("source checkpoint mismatch: {a}, {b}");} Ok(())}

      fn export_path(model:&CruiseHistory<'_,'_>, innovations:&[Innovation])->Result<(CruiseParticle,Vec<Value>,Vec<f64>)> {
          let (mut state, first)=model.extend(None,&innovations[0],0)?;
          let mut factors=vec![first];let mut contacts=Vec::new();
          for (block,input) in innovations[1..].iter().enumerate() {
              let mut rng=ChaCha8Rng::from_seed(input.seed);
              let mut increment=0.0;
              for o in &model.blocks[block] {
                  let prior_bias=state.bfo_bias;
                  let elapsed=o.measurement.time.0-state.flight.aircraft.time.0;
                  let p=model.model.propose(&state,o,elapsed,&mut rng)?;
                  state=p.state;
                  let ll=model.model.observe(&mut state,o)?;
                  increment+=p.log_prior_over_proposal+ll;
                  contacts.push(json!({"observation":o,"prior_bias":prior_bias,"state":state,"log_observation_factor":ll,"log_prior_over_proposal":p.log_prior_over_proposal}));
              }
              factors.push(increment);
          }
          Ok((state,contacts,factors))
      }

      fn main()->Result<()> {
          let args=Arguments::parse();let started=Instant::now();

          for o in observations {let end=o.measurement.bto.is_some();pending.push(o);if end {blocks.push(std::mem::take(&mut pending));}}
          if !pending.is_empty(){blocks.push(pending);}
          if args.through_block>blocks.len(){bail!("invalid endpoint");}
          let history= CruiseHistory{model:&model,blocks:&blocks[..args.through_block],mode_probabilities:serde_json::from_value(cfg["mode_probabilities"].clone())?};

              for (a,b) in result.checkpoints.iter().zip(&checkpoints){close(a.cumulative_log_evidence,b.cumulative_log_evidence)?;close(a.root_effective_sample_size,b.root_effective_sample_size)?;}
              // A deterministic weighted posterior draw, without any truth input.

              let slot=result.log_weights.iter().position(|w|{total+=w.exp();u<total}).unwrap_or(result.particles.len()-1);

              write(&args.output.join("reference.json"),&doc)?;write(&args.output.join("progress.json"),&doc)?;
              println!("prepared exact reference in {:.3}s",started.elapsed().as_secs_f64());return Ok(());
          }
          let reference_path=args.reference.as_ref().unwrap();let reference=read(reference_path)?;
          if reference["source_config_sha256"]!=config_sha || reference["through_block"]!=args.through_block {bail!("reference configuration mismatch");}
          identities.insert(reference_path.to_string_lossy().into_owned(),sha(&fs::read(reference_path)?));
          let mut innovations:Vec<Innovation>=serde_json::from_value(reference["innovations"].clone())?;
          let (initial_state,_,_)=export_path(&history,&innovations)?;
          let old:CruiseParticle=serde_json::from_value(reference["state"].clone())?;
          close(initial_state.flight.aircraft.position.latitude.0,old.flight.aircraft.position.latitude.0)?;
          close(initial_state.cumulative_satcom_log_likelihood,old.cumulative_satcom_log_likelihood)?;
          let mut csv=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("chain.csv"))?);
          writeln!(csv,"iteration,latitude_deg,longitude_deg,altitude_ft,mach,initial_mach,initial_altitude_ft,initial_mode,turns,speed_changes,altitude_changes,log_target_relative_to_proposal,initial_input_changed,changed_blocks,elapsed_seconds")?;
          let mut paths=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("paths.jsonl"))?);
          let mut diagnostic=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("ancestor-diagnostics.jsonl"))?);
          let mut contacts_out=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("contact-checks.jsonl"))?);
          let mut initial_changes=0;let mut block_changes=vec![0;history.steps()];let mut replay_checks=0;
          for iteration in 0..=args.iterations {
              let before=innovations.clone();
              let (state,factors)=if iteration==0 {
                  let (s,_,f)=export_path(&history,&innovations)?;(s,f)
              } else {
                  let result=pool.install(||sample_innovation_history(&history,Some(&innovations),args.particles,args.seed,iteration,!args.without_ancestor_sampling))?;
                  writeln!(diagnostic,"{}",json!({"iteration":iteration,"steps":result.diagnostics}))?;
                  innovations=result.path.innovations;
                  (result.path.state,result.path.log_increments)
              };
              let (replayed,contacts,replayed_factors)=export_path(&history,&innovations)?;
              if state!=replayed || factors!=replayed_factors {bail!("selected path replay mismatch at {iteration}");}
              replay_checks+=1;
              let changed:Vec<bool>=innovations.iter().zip(&before).map(|(a,b)|a!=b).collect();
              initial_changes+=usize::from(changed[0]);
              for (n,c) in block_changes.iter_mut().zip(&changed){*n+=usize::from(*c);}
              let (initial,_)=history.extend(None,&innovations[0],0)?;
              let f=&state.flight;let a=f.aircraft;let total:f64=factors.iter().sum();
              writeln!(csv,"{iteration},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{}",a.position.latitude.0,a.position.longitude.0,a.altitude.0,f.mach_set+f.mach_deviation,initial.flight.mach_set,initial.flight.aircraft.altitude.0,innovations[0].initial_mode.unwrap(),f.manoeuvre_counts[0],f.manoeuvre_counts[1],f.manoeuvre_counts[2],total,changed[0],changed.iter().filter(|c|**c).count(),started.elapsed().as_secs_f64())?;
              writeln!(paths,"{}",json!({"iteration":iteration,"innovations":innovations,"log_increments":factors,"state":state}))?;
              writeln!(contacts_out,"{}",json!({"iteration":iteration,"contacts":contacts}))?;
              csv.flush()?;paths.flush()?;diagnostic.flush()?;contacts_out.flush()?;
              write(&args.output.join("progress.json"),&json!({"status":"running","iteration":iteration,"iterations":args.iterations,"particles":args.particles,"ancestor_sampling":!args.without_ancestor_sampling,"seed":args.seed,"elapsed_seconds":started.elapsed().as_secs_f64(),"initial_input_changes":initial_changes}))?;
          }
          let summary=json!({"status":"completed","source":args.source,"reference":reference_path,"through_block":args.through_block,"fuel_condition_time_s":model.final_time_s,"particles":args.particles,"iterations":args.iterations,"seed":args.seed,"ancestor_sampling":!args.without_ancestor_sampling,"initial_input_changes":initial_changes,"block_input_changes":block_changes,"selected_path_exact_replay_checks":replay_checks,"elapsed_seconds":started.elapsed().as_secs_f64(),"inputs":identities,"code_sha256":sha(include_bytes!("cruise_ancestor_sampling.rs")),"executable_sha256":sha(&fs::read(std::env::current_exe()?)?),"scientific_source_sha256":env!("MH370_SCIENTIFIC_SOURCE_SHA256"),"chain_sha256":sha(&fs::read(args.output.join("chain.csv"))?),"scope":"A conditional path Markov-chain control through the selected original observation block. All full-suffix factors retained. Chain states are dependent, not independent flights or an evidence estimate. Original physical priors and observations unchanged; no generating truth used."});
          write(&args.output.join("summary.json"),&summary)?;write(&args.output.join("progress.json"),&summary)?;
          println!("{}",summary);
          Ok(())
      }

      """Fixed, recorded diagnostic queue; never repeat an existing output directory."""
      from pathlib import Path
      from datetime import datetime, timezone
      import os, subprocess, json, time

      ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
      WEB = ROOT.parent.parent / 'overnight-analysis' / 'web'
      EXE = ROOT / 'cruise-ancestor-sampling'
      SOURCE = ROOT.parent / 'synthetic-cruise-fuel-recovery' / 'seed-37092100'
      JOBS = []
      for seed in [37102100, 37102101]:
          JOBS.append((f'reference-{seed}', ['--source', str(SOURCE/f'inference-seed-{seed}')]))
      JOBS.append(('one-particle-compatibility', ['--source', str(SOURCE/'inference-seed-37102100'), '--reference', str(ROOT/'reference-37102100/reference.json'), '--particles', '1', '--iterations', '1', '--seed', '37118100']))
      for original, seed in [(37102100,37118101),(37102101,37118102)]:
          for method in ['ancestor','ordinary']:
              args=['--source',str(SOURCE/f'inference-seed-{original}'),'--reference',str(ROOT/f'reference-{original}/reference.json'),'--particles','64','--iterations','32','--seed',str(seed)]
              if method == 'ordinary':args+=['--without-ancestor-sampling']
              JOBS.append((f'{method}-{seed}',args))


      def write_status(record):
          record['checked_utc']=datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
          record['driver_pid']=os.getpid()
          (ROOT/'progress.json').write_text(json.dumps(record,indent=2)+'\n')
          (WEB/'mh370-running-status.json').write_text(json.dumps({
              'checked_utc':record['checked_utc'], 'comparison_finished':record['status']=='completed',
              'jobs':[{'title':'Cruise history-revision control: '+record.get('current_job',record['status']),
                       'recorded_status':record['status'],'driver_alive':record['status']=='running',
                       'worker_alive':record['status']=='running','worker_pid':record.get('worker_pid'),
                       'completed_runs':len(record['completed']),'planned_runs':len(JOBS),
                       'sample_unit':'diagnostic preparation and conditional Markov-chain controls; not new complete MH370 trajectories'}]
          },indent=2)+'\n')


      def main():
          state={'status':'running','started_utc':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),'completed':[],'planned_jobs':[n for n,_ in JOBS]}
          for name,args in JOBS:
              output=ROOT/name
              if output.exists():raise RuntimeError(f'Existing output requires inspection: {output}')
              command=[str(EXE),*args,'--output',str(output),'--threads','4']
              started=time.monotonic()
              with (ROOT/(name+'.log')).open('w') as log:
                  child=subprocess.Popen(command,stdout=log,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
                  state.update({'current_job':name,'worker_pid':child.pid,'current_command':command})
                  write_status(state)
                  while True:
                      pid,status,usage=os.wait4(child.pid,os.WNOHANG)
                      if pid:break
                      time.sleep(1)
                      write_status(state)
              code=os.waitstatus_to_exitcode(status);child.returncode=code
              resource={'name':name,'command':command,'exit_code':code,'elapsed_seconds':time.monotonic()-started,'peak_rss_kib':usage.ru_maxrss,'user_cpu_s':usage.ru_utime,'system_cpu_s':usage.ru_stime}
              (ROOT/(name+'-resources.json')).write_text(json.dumps(resource,indent=2)+'\n')
              if code:
                  state.update({'status':'failed','failed_job':name,'resource':resource});write_status(state)
                  raise RuntimeError(f'{name} failed with exit {code}; inspect log, no automatic retry')
              state['completed'].append(resource)
              print(json.dumps(resource),flush=True)
          state.update({'status':'completed','finished_utc':datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),'current_job':None,'worker_pid':None})
          write_status(state)


      if __name__=='__main__':main()

      """Independent Gaussian marginalization and saved-chain target checks."""
      from pathlib import Path
      import json, csv, math, hashlib
      import numpy as np

      ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
      def read(p):return json.loads(p.read_text())
      def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
      def cdf_gap(a,b):
          e=np.unique(np.r_[a,b])
          return float(np.max(abs(np.searchsorted(np.sort(a),e,side='right')/len(a)-np.searchsorted(np.sort(b),e,side='right')/len(b))))

      records=[];maximum_gaussian_error=0.;maximum_bias_error=0.;maximum_dense_error=0.;contacts_checked=0
      for out in sorted(ROOT.glob('*-3711810[12]')):
          summary=read(out/'summary.json')
          assert summary['status']=='completed'
          for p,h in summary['inputs'].items():assert sha(p)==h
          assert sha(out/'chain.csv')==summary['chain_sha256']
          csv_rows=list(csv.DictReader((out/'chain.csv').open()))
          paths=[json.loads(s) for s in (out/'paths.jsonl').open()]
          contacts=[json.loads(s) for s in (out/'contact-checks.jsonl').open()]
          assert len(csv_rows)==len(paths)==len(contacts)==summary['iterations']+1
          for row,path,record in zip(csv_rows,paths,contacts):
              bto_total=0.;bfo_total=0.;correction=0.;residuals=[];variances=[]
              mean=150.;variance=625.
              for contact in record['contacts']:
                  state=contact['state'];fit=state['last_fit'];m=contact['observation']['measurement']
                  before=contact['prior_bias'];ll=0.
                  assert abs(before['mean_hz']-mean)<1e-10
                  assert abs(before['variance_hz2']-variance)<1e-10
                  if m['bto'] is not None:
                      value=-.5*((m['bto']-fit['predicted_bto_us'])/m['bto_sd'])**2-math.log(m['bto_sd']*math.sqrt(2*math.pi))
                      bto_total+=value;ll+=value
                  if m['bfo'] is not None:
                      raw=m['bfo']-fit['predicted_bfo_without_bias_hz'];e=raw-mean;s=variance+m['bfo_sd']**2
                      value=-.5*(e*e/s+math.log(2*math.pi*s));ll+=value;bfo_total+=value
                      mean+=variance/s*e;variance*=m['bfo_sd']**2/s
                      residuals.append(raw-150.);variances.append(m['bfo_sd']**2)
                  error=abs(ll-contact['log_observation_factor']);maximum_gaussian_error=max(maximum_gaussian_error,error);assert error<1e-8
                  maximum_bias_error=max(maximum_bias_error,abs(mean-state['bfo_bias']['mean_hz']),abs(variance-state['bfo_bias']['variance_hz2']))
                  assert maximum_bias_error<1e-10
                  assert state['fuel_log_factor'] is None, 'Fuel was incorrectly moved to the short diagnostic endpoint'
                  correction+=contact['log_prior_over_proposal'];contacts_checked+=1
              covariance=np.diag(variances)+625.*np.ones((len(variances),len(variances)))
              sign,logdet=np.linalg.slogdet(covariance);assert sign==1
              dense=-.5*(np.array(residuals)@np.linalg.solve(covariance,residuals)+logdet+len(residuals)*math.log(2*math.pi))
              maximum_dense_error=max(maximum_dense_error,abs(dense-bfo_total));assert maximum_dense_error<1e-8
              expected=bto_total+dense+correction
              assert abs(expected-sum(path['log_increments']))<1e-8
              assert abs(expected-float(row['log_target_relative_to_proposal']))<1e-8
          n=len(csv_rows)
          initial_mach=np.array([float(r['initial_mach']) for r in csv_rows]);alt=np.array([float(r['initial_altitude_ft']) for r in csv_rows]);mode=np.array([int(r['initial_mode']) for r in csv_rows])
          tail=csv_rows[n//2:]
          records.append({'run':out.name,'ancestor_sampling':summary['ancestor_sampling'],'seed':summary['seed'],'elapsed_seconds':summary['elapsed_seconds'],
            'initial_input_changes':summary['initial_input_changes'],'initial_mach_changes':int(np.sum(initial_mach[1:]!=initial_mach[:-1])),
            'initial_altitude_changes':int(np.sum(alt[1:]!=alt[:-1])),'initial_mode_changes':int(np.sum(mode[1:]!=mode[:-1])),
            'distinct_initial_modes':np.unique(mode).tolist(),'distinct_initial_altitudes_ft':np.unique(alt).tolist(),
            'latitude_range_deg':[min(float(r['latitude_deg']) for r in csv_rows),max(float(r['latitude_deg']) for r in csv_rows)],
            'last_half_latitudes':[float(r['latitude_deg']) for r in tail],'last_half_longitudes':[float(r['longitude_deg']) for r in tail],
            'initial_log_target':float(csv_rows[0]['log_target_relative_to_proposal']),'final_log_target':float(csv_rows[-1]['log_target_relative_to_proposal']),
            'summary_sha256':sha(out/'summary.json'),'path_sha256':sha(out/'paths.jsonl'),'contacts_sha256':sha(out/'contact-checks.jsonl')})
      comparisons=[]
      for asampling in [False,True]:
          pair=[r for r in records if r['ancestor_sampling']==asampling]
          if len(pair)==2:comparisons.append({'ancestor_sampling':asampling,'last_half_coordinate_cdf_gaps':[cdf_gap(pair[0][key],pair[1][key]) for key in ['last_half_latitudes','last_half_longitudes']]})
      result={'status':'passed','contacts_checked':contacts_checked,'maximum_gaussian_log_error':maximum_gaussian_error,'maximum_bias_error':maximum_bias_error,'maximum_dense_bfo_log_error':maximum_dense_error,'records':records,'comparisons':comparisons,'scope':'Short dependent Markov chains; descriptive exploration diagnostics, not convergence, burn-in certification or new independent MH370 trajectories.'}
      (ROOT/'independent-verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
      print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k!='records'}))
      for record in records:print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in record.items() if not k.startswith('last_half') and not k.endswith('sha256')}))

     x=np.genfromtxt(path/'chain.csv',delimiter=',',names=True,dtype=None,encoding='utf-8')

I’m checking an exact way to help the chains cross between alternatives: auxiliary chains with softened likelihoods and probability-corrected exchanges. The final chain would retain the original observations and physical assumptions.

    ── Created .sources/lindsten-2014-particle-gibbs-ancestor-sampling/code/replica_exchange_reference.py +58
      """Independent exact check of likelihood-only tempering plus PGAS exchanges.

      Our extension of the recreated PGAS kernel, not a claim from the cited paper.
      Use physical likelihood L = (original toy likelihood)**2, so beta=1/2 and 1
      both have rational targets. Physical prior / proposal ratios are never raised
      to beta. Enumerate each PGAS transition and every exchange detailed-balance
      identity, then compose their invariant distributions.
      """
      from fractions import Fraction as F
      from pathlib import Path
      from finite_reference import PATHS, Q, likelihood, exact_target, transition
      import json


      def main():
          targets = []
          errors = []
          for power in [1, 2]:
              target, _ = exact_target(power)
              matrix = [transition(p, 'pgas', power) for p in PATHS]
              after = [sum(target[i]*matrix[i][j] for i in range(8)) for j in range(8)]
              errors.append(max(abs(a-b) for a, b in zip(target, after)))
              targets.append(target)
          raw_likelihood = []
          for p in PATHS:
              l = F(1)
              for step in range(3):
                  l *= likelihood(p, step)
              raw_likelihood.append(l)
          # Coordinates are (hot path i, cold path j). The full physical likelihood
          # is raw_likelihood**2 and beta_cold-beta_hot is 1/2.
          target = [targets[0][i]*targets[1][j] for i in range(8) for j in range(8)]
          after = [F(0)]*64
          wrong = [F(0)]*64
          balance = F(0)
          for i in range(8):
              for j in range(8):
                  a, b = i*8+j, j*8+i
                  ratio = raw_likelihood[i]/raw_likelihood[j]
                  accept = min(F(1), ratio)
                  reverse = min(F(1), 1/ratio)
                  balance = max(balance, abs(target[a]*accept-target[b]*reverse))
                  after[a] += target[a]*(1-accept)
                  after[b] += target[a]*accept
                  wrong[a] += target[a]*(1-reverse)
                  wrong[b] += target[a]*reverse
          error = max(abs(a-b) for a,b in zip(after,target))
          wrong_error = max(abs(a-b) for a,b in zip(wrong,target))
          assert max(errors) == balance == error == 0
          assert wrong_error > 0
          result = dict(status='passed', pg_target_invariance_errors=list(map(str,errors)),
              exchange_detailed_balance_error=str(balance), joint_invariance_error=str(error),
              reversed_ratio_invariance_error=float(wrong_error),
              betas=['1/2','1'], proposal_probabilities=list(map(str,Q)),
              hot_target=list(map(str,targets[0])), cold_target=list(map(str,targets[1])),
              scope=__doc__)
          output=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]/'outputs/replica-exchange-reference.json'
          output.write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
          print(json.dumps(result))


      if __name__=='__main__':
          main()

    ── Edited /tmp/mh370-cruise-ancestor-sampling/crates/particle-filter/src/ancestor_sampling.rs +29
      @@ -9,2 +9,31 @@
       
      +/// Likelihood-only bridge: never soften the physical-prior/proposal ratio.
      +/// The unmodified scientific posterior is the beta=1 replica.
      +pub fn likelihood_power_increment(log_prior_over_proposal: f64, log_likelihood: f64, beta: f64) -> f64 {
      +    log_prior_over_proposal + beta * log_likelihood
      +}
      +
      +/// Swap two full histories between fixed likelihood powers. Physical priors
      +/// and the common innovation reference cancel in the exchange ratio.
      +pub fn likelihood_exchange_log_acceptance(beta_a: f64, beta_b: f64, likelihood_a: f64, likelihood_b: f64) -> f64 {
      +    ((beta_a-beta_b)*(likelihood_b-likelihood_a)).min(0.0)
      +}
      +
      +#[cfg(test)]
      +mod likelihood_power_tests {
      +    use super::*;
      +    #[test]
      +    fn unequal_physical_prior_is_untempered_and_exchange_has_correct_direction() {
      +        let p_over_q: f64=2.0/7.0;
      +        let likelihood: f64=9.0/100.0;
      +        let expected: f64=3.0/35.0;
      +        assert!((likelihood_power_increment(p_over_q.ln(),likelihood.ln(),0.5).exp()-expected).abs()<1e-15);
      +        // Hot a carries likelihood .04, cold b carries .81. Swapping gives
      +        // (.04/.81)^(1-.5)=2/9, independently calculated in probability space.
      +        let accept=likelihood_exchange_log_acceptance(0.5,1.0,0.04_f64.ln(),0.81_f64.ln()).exp();
