    fn run_replicas(args:&Arguments,history:&CruiseHistory<'_,'_>,initial:Vec<Innovation>,identities:BTreeMap<String,String>,pool:&rayon::ThreadPool,started:Instant)->Result<()> {
        let mut replicas=vec"'![initial;args.likelihood_powers.len()];
        let cold=replicas.len()-1;
        let mut csv=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("chain.csv"))?);
        writeln!(csv,"iteration,latitude_deg,longitude_deg,altitude_ft,mach,initial_mach,initial_altitude_ft,initial_mode,turns,speed_changes,altitude_changes,log_target_relative_to_proposal,initial_input_changed,changed_blocks,elapsed_seconds")?;
        let mut paths=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("paths.jsonl"))?);
        let mut diagnostic=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("ancestor-diagnostics.jsonl"))?);
        let mut contacts_out=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("contact-checks.jsonl"))?);
        let mut replica_out=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("replica-checks.jsonl"))?);
        let mut exchanges_out=BufWriter::new(fs::File::create(args.output.join("exchanges.jsonl"))?);
        let mut initial_changes=0;let mut block_changes=vec![0;history.steps()];
        let mut swaps=vec![0;replicas.len()-1];let mut attempts=vec![0;replicas.len()-1];
        for iteration in 0..=args.iterations {
            let before=replicas[cold].clone();
            let mut log_likelihoods=Vec::new();
            for replica in 0..replicas.len() {
                let beta=args.likelihood_powers[replica];
                let helper=CruiseHistory{model:history.model,blocks:history.blocks,mode_probabilities:history.mode_probabilities.clone(),likelihood_power:beta};
                let tempered_factors=if iteration>0 {
                    let key=derive_seed(args.seed,"likelihood-replica",0,replica,0);
                    let seed=u64::from_le_bytes(key[..8].try_into().unwrap());
                    let result=pool.install(||sample_innovation_history(&helper,Some(&replicas[replica]),args.particles,seed,iteration,true))?;
                    writeln!(diagnostic,"{}",json!({"iteration":iteration,"replica":replica,"likelihood_power":beta,"steps":result.diagnostics}))?;
                    replicas[replica]=result.path.innovations;
                    result.path.log_increments
                } else {
                    let mut state=None;let mut factors=Vec::new();
                    for (step,input) in replicas[replica].iter().enumerate(){let (s,f)=helper.extend(state.as_ref(),input,step)?;state=Some(s);factors.push(f);}
                    factors
                };
                let (state,contacts,untempered_factors)=export_path(history,&replicas[replica])?;
                let likelihood=state.cumulative_satcom_log_likelihood+state.fuel_log_factor.unwrap_or(0.0);
                let original_target=untempered_factors.iter().sum::<f64>();
                let tempered_target=tempered_factors.iter().sum::<f64>();
                close(tempered_target,original_target+(beta-1.0)*likelihood)?;
                log_likelihoods.push(likelihood);
                writeln!(replica_out,"{}",json!({"iteration":iteration,"replica":replica,"likelihood_power":beta,"innovations":replicas[replica],"state":state,"contacts":contacts,"log_likelihood":likelihood,"tempered_log_increments":tempered_factors,"untempered_log_increments":untempered_factors}))?;
            }
            let mut exchange_records=Vec::new();
            if iteration>0 {
                // Alternating disjoint pairs are a fixed composition of individually
                // invariant swaps. No adaptive ladder or selected generating truth.
                for a in ((iteration-1)%2..cold).step_by(2) {
                    let b=a+1;
                    let log_accept=likelihood_exchange_log_acceptance(args.likelihood_powers[a],args.likelihood_powers[b],log_likelihoods[a],log_likelihoods[b]);
                    let mut rng=rng_for(args.seed,"likelihood-replica-exchange",iteration,a,0);
                    let u=rng.gen::<f64>();let accepted=u.ln()<log_accept;
                    attempts[a]+=1;swaps[a]+=usize::from(accepted);
                    exchange_records.push(json!({"a":a,"b":b,"beta_a":args.likelihood_powers[a],"beta_b":args.likelihood_powers[b],"log_likelihood_a":log_likelihoods[a],"log_likelihood_b":log_likelihoods[b],"log_acceptance":log_accept,"uniform":u,"accepted":accepted}));
                    if accepted {replicas.swap(a,b);log_likelihoods.swap(a,b);}
                }
            }
            writeln!(exchanges_out,"{}",json!({"iteration":iteration,"exchanges":exchange_records}))?;
            let innovations=&replicas[cold];
            let (state,contacts,factors)=export_path(history,innovations)?;
            let changed:Vec<bool>=innovations.iter().zip(&before).map(|(a,b)|a!=b).collect();
            initial_changes+=usize::from(changed[0]);
            for (n,c) in block_changes.iter_mut().zip(&changed){*n+=usize::from(*c);}
            let (initial,_)=history.extend(None,&innovations[0],0)?;
            let f=&state.flight;let a=f.aircraft;let total:f64=factors.iter().sum();
            close(state.cumulative_satcom_log_likelihood+state.fuel_log_factor.unwrap_or(0.0),log_likelihoods[cold])?;
            writeln!(csv,"{iteration},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{},{}",a.position.latitude.0,a.position.longitude.0,a.altitude.0,f.mach_set+f.mach_deviation,initial.flight.mach_set,initial.flight.aircraft.altitude.0,innovations[0].initial_mode.unwrap(),f.manoeuvre_counts[0],f.manoeuvre_counts[1],f.manoeuvre_counts[2],total,changed[0],changed.iter().filter(|c|**c).count(),started.elapsed().as_secs_f64())?;
            writeln!(paths,"{}",json!({"iteration":iteration,"innovations":innovations,"log_increments":factors,"state":state}))?;
            writeln!(contacts_out,"{}",json!({"iteration":iteration,"contacts":contacts}))?;
            csv.flush()?;paths.flush()?;diagnostic.flush()?;contacts_out.flush()?;replica_out.flush()?;exchanges_out.flush()?;
            write(&args.output.join("progress.json"),&json!({"status":"running","iteration":iteration,"iterations":args.iterations,"particles":args.particles,"ancestor_sampling":true,"seed":args.seed,"likelihood_powers":args.likelihood_powers,"elapsed_seconds":started.elapsed().as_secs_f64(),"initial_input_changes":initial_changes,"exchanges_accepted":swaps,"exchanges_attempted":attempts}))?;
        }
        let summary=json!({"status":"completed","source":args.source,"reference":args.reference,"through_block":args.through_block,"fuel_condition_time_s":history.model.final_time_s,"particles":args.particles,"iterations":args.iterations,"seed":args.seed,"ancestor_sampling":true,"likelihood_powers":args.likelihood_powers,"initial_input_changes":initial_changes,"block_input_changes":block_changes,"selected_path_exact_replay_checks":args.iterations+1,"exchanges_accepted":swaps,"exchanges_attempted":attempts,"elapsed_seconds":started.elapsed().as_secs_f64(),"inputs":identities,"code_sha256":sha(include_bytes!("cruise_ancestor_sampling.rs")),"executable_sha256":sha(&fs::read(std::env::current_exe()?)?),"scientific_source_sha256":env!("MH370_SCIENTIFIC_SOURCE_SHA256"),"chain_sha256":sha(&fs::read(args.output.join("chain.csv"))?),"scope":"Fixed likelihood-only replicas with exact full-suffix PGAS and alternating exact exchanges. Exported chain has beta=1; physical priors, marginal observation likelihood and source data unchanged. Dependent diagnostic samples, not independent flights or an evidence estimate."});
        write(&args.output.join("summary.json"),&summary)?;write(&args.output.join("progress.json"),&summary)?;println!("{}",summary);Ok(())
    }

      $ cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/thr_rzreetwcyu/branching-filter-analysis/cruise-ancestor-sampling/verify_exchanges.py <<'PY'
    """Independent saved-data check of helper targets, exchange balance and cold output."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import csv,hashlib,json,math
    import numpy as np
    ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent

    def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    def read(p):return json.loads(p.read_text())
    def lines(p):return [json.loads(s) for s in p.open()]
    def gap(a,b):
     e=np.unique(np.r_[a,b]);return float(np.max(abs(np.searchsorted(np.sort(a),e,side='right')/len(a)-np.searchsorted(np.sort(b),e,side='right')/len(b))))

    def likelihood(contacts):
     mean=150.;variance=625.;total=0.;block=0.;blocks=[];errors=[];residuals=[];variances=[];bfo_total=0.;bto_total=0.
     for c in contacts:
      s=c['state'];fit=s['last_fit'];m=c['observation']['measurement'];before=c['prior_bias'];ll=0.
      assert abs(before['mean_hz']-mean)<1e-9 and abs(before['variance_hz2']-variance)<1e-9
      if m['bto'] is not None:
       x=-.5*((m['bto']-fit['predicted_bto_us'])/m['bto_sd'])**2-math.log(m['bto_sd']*math.sqrt(2*math.pi));ll+=x;bto_total+=x
      if m['bfo'] is not None:
       raw=m['bfo']-fit['predicted_bfo_without_bias_hz'];res=raw-mean;v=variance+m['bfo_sd']**2
       x=-.5*(res*res/v+math.log(2*math.pi*v));ll+=x;bfo_total+=x
       residuals.append(raw-150.);variances.append(m['bfo_sd']**2)
       k=variance/v;mean+=k*res;variance*=1-k
      errors.append(abs(ll-c['log_observation_factor']));assert max(errors)<1e-8
      assert abs(mean-s['bfo_bias']['mean_hz'])<1e-9 and abs(variance-s['bfo_bias']['variance_hz2'])<1e-9
      assert s['fuel_log_factor'] is None
      total+=ll;block+=ll
      if m['bto'] is not None:blocks.append(block);block=0.
     covariance=np.diag(variances)+625*np.ones((len(variances),len(variances)))
     sign,det=np.linalg.slogdet(covariance);assert sign==1
     dense=-.5*(np.array(residuals)@np.linalg.solve(covariance,residuals)+det+len(variances)*math.log(2*math.pi))
     assert abs(dense-bfo_total)<1e-8
     return total,np.array([0.]+blocks),max(errors+[abs(dense-bfo_total)])

    records=[];contacts_count=0;target_count=0;exchange_count=0;max_ll=0.;max_target=0.;max_exchange=0.
    for out in sorted(ROOT.glob('exchange-ancestor-*')):
     if not out.is_dir():continue
     summary=read(out/'summary.json');assert summary['status']=='completed'
     for p,h in summary['inputs'].items():assert sha(p)==h
     assert sha(out/'chain.csv')==summary['chain_sha256']
     rows=list(csv.DictReader((out/'chain.csv').open()));cold_paths=lines(out/'paths.jsonl');exchanges=lines(out/'exchanges.jsonl')
     replicas=lines(out/'replica-checks.jsonl');nr=len(summary['likelihood_powers'])
     assert len(replicas)==nr*(summary['iterations']+1)
     swaps=[0]*(nr-1);attempts=swaps.copy()
     for iteration in range(summary['iterations']+1):
      current=replicas[iteration*nr:(iteration+1)*nr]
      for j,r in enumerate(current):
       assert r['iteration']==iteration and r['replica']==j
       ll,block_ll,error=likelihood(r['contacts']);contacts_count+=len(r['contacts']);max_ll=max(max_ll,error)
       assert abs(ll-r['log_likelihood'])<1e-8
       raw=np.array(r['untempered_log_increments']);powered=np.array(r['tempered_log_increments'])
       expected=raw+(r['likelihood_power']-1.)*block_ll
       error=float(np.max(abs(expected-powered)));max_target=max(max_target,error);assert error<1e-8
       # The physical-prior/proposal factor is the unchanged difference.
       correction=raw-block_ll
       assert np.max(abs(powered-r['likelihood_power']*block_ll-correction))<1e-8
       target_count+=len(raw)
      e=exchanges[iteration];assert e['iteration']==iteration
      for swap in e['exchanges']:
       a,b=swap['a'],swap['b'];pa,pb=current[a],current[b];ba,bb=summary['likelihood_powers'][a:b+1]
       la,lb=pa['log_likelihood'],pb['log_likelihood']
       assert abs(la-swap['log_likelihood_a'])<1e-9 and abs(lb-swap['log_likelihood_b'])<1e-9
       ca=sum(pa['untempered_log_increments'])-la;cb=sum(pb['untempered_log_increments'])-lb
       before=ca+ba*la+cb+bb*lb;after=cb+ba*lb+ca+bb*la
       expected=min(0.,after-before);max_exchange=max(max_exchange,abs(expected-swap['log_acceptance']));assert max_exchange<1e-9
       accepted=math.log(swap['uniform'])<expected
       assert accepted==swap['accepted'];attempts[a]+=1;swaps[a]+=int(accepted);exchange_count+=1
       if accepted:current[a],current[b]=current[b],current[a]
      selected=current[-1];cold=cold_paths[iteration]
      assert selected['innovations']==cold['innovations'] and selected['state']==cold['state']
      assert selected['untempered_log_increments']==cold['log_increments']
      assert abs(sum(cold['log_increments'])-float(rows[iteration]['log_target_relative_to_proposal']))<1e-8
     assert swaps==summary['exchanges_accepted'] and attempts==summary['exchanges_attempted']
     tail=rows[len(rows)//2:]

It is not a second product implementation or a new MH370 posterior. The frozen source/executables and independent data checks live here; uncertain code is in "'`/tmp/mh370-cruise-ancestor-sampling` until assessed. The model is unchanged. Its initial mode probabilities, Mach/altitude priors, command processes, observation uncertainties, marginalized BFO bias and fuel propagation are retained. The complete observation schedule sets the fuel condition at 00:11; these short controls stop at 19:41 and do not score fuel prematurely. No generating-truth files enter inference. The new transition is conditional particle Gibbs with exact ancestor sampling in simulator-innovation space. For each possible earlier history, all remaining retained innovations are replayed and their full likelihood and prior/proposal factors recomputed. Thus later data can revise an earlier physical history. No truncated suffix, physical-state splicing or coordinate-only replay move is used. The archived full-history and ordered forward/backward variants did not implement this transition. Independent exact-rational recreation: repository `.sources/lindsten-2014-particle-gibbs-ancestor-sampling/README.md` (Lindsten, Jordan & Schön 2014, Algorithm 2, eqs.3/22). The generic kernel’s independent transition-row and worker-count checks pass. Flight path, correlated BFO likelihood and complete-suffix checks are in `independent-verification.json`. The 32-sweep pilots changed initial physical conditions 13/7 times; matched ordinary particle Gibbs made no changes. Continuing each for 512 sweeps made 79/229 more initial changes, but last-half coordinate CDFs still differ by 58.37 percentage points. This establishes an ability to revise histories, not satisfactory posterior exploration, recovery or convergence. `mixing-diagnosis.json` identifies near-single-predecessor mass at the second observation block. `exchange-design.json` defines the next correction: fixed likelihood-only replica exchanges around the exact PGAS kernel. The beta=1 chain has the original target. Auxiliary chains use the same physical prior and proposal correction, with likelihood powers 1/16, 1/8, 1/4 and 1/2. Exact independent exchange invariance and default-target compatibility passed before running the paired 128-sweep controls. Their results must be checked using `verify_exchanges.py` before assessment or reporting. Provenance and reproduction: - `design.json`: baseline, first prototype and full-suffix-check source hashes. - `exchange-design.json`: later source/executable hashes, fixed protocol and   mathematical correction; the initial references and all input identities are   included in each run summary. - `execute.py`, `continue_chains.py`, `run_exchanges.py`: exact recorded queues.     # Exact revision of earlier flight histories

    This is the owner’s isolated implementation investigation, begun 18 September
    2026. It is not a second product implementation or a new MH370 posterior.
    The frozen source/executables and independent data checks live here; uncertain
    code is in "'`/tmp/mh370-cruise-ancestor-sampling` until assessed.

    The model is unchanged. Its initial mode probabilities, Mach/altitude priors,
    command processes, observation uncertainties, marginalized BFO bias and fuel
    propagation are retained. The complete observation schedule sets the fuel
    condition at 00:11; these short controls stop at 19:41 and do not score fuel
    prematurely. No generating-truth files enter inference.

    The new transition is conditional particle Gibbs with exact ancestor sampling
    in simulator-innovation space. For each possible earlier history, all remaining
    retained innovations are replayed and their full likelihood and prior/proposal
    factors recomputed. Thus later data can revise an earlier physical history.
    No truncated suffix, physical-state splicing or coordinate-only replay move is
    used. The archived full-history and ordered forward/backward variants did not
    implement this transition.

    Independent exact-rational recreation: repository
    `.sources/lindsten-2014-particle-gibbs-ancestor-sampling/README.md` (Lindsten,
    Jordan & Schön 2014, Algorithm 2, eqs.3/22). The generic kernel’s independent
    transition-row and worker-count checks pass. Flight path, correlated BFO
    likelihood and complete-suffix checks are in `independent-verification.json`.

    The 32-sweep pilots changed initial physical conditions 13/7 times; matched
    ordinary particle Gibbs made no changes. Continuing each for 512 sweeps made
    79/229 more initial changes, but last-half coordinate CDFs still differ by
    58.37 percentage points. This establishes an ability to revise histories,
    not satisfactory posterior exploration, recovery or convergence.

    `mixing-diagnosis.json` identifies near-single-predecessor mass at the second
    observation block. `exchange-design.json` defines the next correction: fixed
    likelihood-only replica exchanges around the exact PGAS kernel. The beta=1
    chain has the original target. Auxiliary chains use the same physical prior
    and proposal correction, with likelihood powers 1/16, 1/8, 1/4 and 1/2.
    Exact independent exchange invariance and default-target compatibility passed
    before running the paired 128-sweep controls. Their results must be checked
    using `verify_exchanges.py` before assessment or reporting.

    Provenance and reproduction:

    - `design.json`: baseline, first prototype and full-suffix-check source hashes.
    - `exchange-design.json`: later source/executable hashes, fixed protocol and
      mathematical correction; the initial references and all input identities are
      included in each run summary.
    - `execute.py`, `continue_chains.py`, `run_exchanges.py`: exact recorded queues.

    - `verify.py`, `verify_exchanges.py`: saved-data independent calculations; these

    are labelled synthetic diagnostics; existing MH370 impact/drift/search weights

      $ cat > /jackbox/home/.iso/thread-storage/thr_rzreetwcyu/branching-filter-analysis/cruise-ancestor-sampling/verify_suffix.py <<'PY'
    """Independent decomposition of exactly reproduced full-suffix ancestor weights."""
    from pathlib import Path
    import csv,hashlib,json,math
    import numpy as np
    ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parent

    def read(p):return json.loads(Path(p).read_text())
    def rows(p):return [json.loads(x) for x in Path(p).open()]
    def sha(p):return hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()
    def weights(x):
     p=np.exp(np.asarray(x)-np.max(x));return p/p.sum()
    def ess(x):
     p=weights(x);return float(1/np.sum(p*p))
    records=[];contacts_checked=0;maximum_ll_error=0.;maximum_weight_error=0.
    for ref in read(ROOT/'suffix-diagnosis-plan.json')['references']:
     seed=ref['seed'];iteration=ref['iteration'];out=ROOT/f'suffix-replay-{seed}';summary=read(out/'summary.json')
     assert summary['status']=='completed' and summary['fuel_condition_time_s']==22150.
     for p,h in summary['inputs'].items():assert sha(p)==h
     original=Path(ref['expected_original_path']).parent
     expected=next(r for r in rows(original/'paths.jsonl') if r['iteration']==iteration)
     actual=rows(out/'paths.jsonl')[-1]
     for key in ['innovations','state','log_increments']:assert actual[key]==expected[key],key
     old=next(r for r in rows(original/'ancestor-diagnostics.jsonl') if r['iteration']==iteration)
     new=rows(out/'ancestor-diagnostics.jsonl')[0]
     assert new['kernel_iteration']==iteration and new['steps']==old['steps']
     raw=read(out/'suffix-components.json')['records'];assert len(raw)==64
     incoming=[];like=[];correction=[];full=[];coordinates=[]
     for row in raw:
      initial=row['prefix_state']['bfo_bias'];mean=initial['mean_hz'];variance=initial['variance_hz2'];initial_mean=mean;initial_var=variance
      l=[];q=[];residuals=[];variances=[];bfo=0.
      assert row['ancestor_log_weight']==old['steps'][2]['ancestor_log_weights'][row['index']]
      for c in row['contacts']:
       before=c['before'];state=c['state'];m=c['observation']['measurement'];fit=state['last_fit'];ll=0.
       assert abs(before['bfo_bias']['mean_hz']-mean)<1e-9 and abs(before['bfo_bias']['variance_hz2']-variance)<1e-9
       if m['bto'] is not None:ll+=-.5*((m['bto']-fit['predicted_bto_us'])/m['bto_sd'])**2-math.log(m['bto_sd']*math.sqrt(2*math.pi))
       if m['bfo'] is not None:
        residual=m['bfo']-fit['predicted_bfo_without_bias_hz'];residuals.append(residual-initial_mean);variances.append(m['bfo_sd']**2)
        error=residual-mean;v=variance+m['bfo_sd']**2;x=-.5*(error*error/v+math.log(2*math.pi*v));ll+=x;bfo+=x
        k=variance/v;mean+=k*error;variance*=1-k
       assert state['fuel_log_factor'] is None
       err=abs(ll-c['log_observation_factor']);maximum_ll_error=max(maximum_ll_error,err);assert err<1e-8
       assert abs(mean-state['bfo_bias']['mean_hz'])<1e-9 and abs(variance-state['bfo_bias']['variance_hz2'])<1e-9
       l.append(ll);q.append(c['log_prior_over_proposal']);contacts_checked+=1
      cov=np.diag(variances)+initial_var*np.ones((len(variances),len(variances)));sign,det=np.linalg.slogdet(cov);assert sign==1
      dense=-.5*(np.array(residuals)@np.linalg.solve(cov,residuals)+det+len(variances)*math.log(2*math.pi));assert abs(dense-bfo)<1e-8
      computed=row['incoming_normalized_log_weight']+sum(l)+sum(q)
      err=abs(computed-row['ancestor_log_weight']);maximum_weight_error=max(maximum_weight_error,err);assert err<1e-7
      incoming.append(row['incoming_normalized_log_weight']);like.append(l);correction.append(q);full.append(row['ancestor_log_weight'])
      coordinates.append([row['prefix_state']['flight']['aircraft']['position']['latitude'],row['terminal_state']['flight']['aircraft']['position']['latitude']])
     incoming=np.array(incoming);like=np.array(like);correction=np.array(correction);full=np.array(full)
     assert abs(np.exp(incoming).sum()-1)<1e-10
     times=[c['observation']['measurement']['time'] for c in raw[0]['contacts']]
     p=weights(full);ordered=np.argsort(full)[::-1];best,second=map(int,ordered[:2]);contrast=[{'time_s':t,'likelihood_log_advantage':float(like[best,j]-like[second,j]),'proposal_correction_log_advantage':float(correction[best,j]-correction[second,j])} for j,t in enumerate(times)]
     cumulative=[]
     for j,t in enumerate(times):
      cumulative.append({'time_s':t,'observation_only_ess':ess(incoming+like[:,:j+1].sum(axis=1)),'correction_only_ess':ess(incoming+correction[:,:j+1].sum(axis=1)),'combined_ess':ess(incoming+like[:,:j+1].sum(axis=1)+correction[:,:j+1].sum(axis=1))})
     record={'run':out.name,'seed':seed,'iteration':iteration,'exact_saved_update_reproduced':True,'summary_sha256':sha(out/'summary.json'),'components_sha256':sha(out/'suffix-components.json'),'incoming_ess':ess(incoming),'observation_only_ess':ess(incoming+like.sum(axis=1)),'correction_only_ess':ess(incoming+correction.sum(axis=1)),'full_ancestor_ess':ess(full),'maximum_ancestor_mass':float(max(p)),'largest_ancestor_index':best,'second_ancestor_index':second,'cumulative_components':cumulative,'best_vs_second':contrast,'incoming_log_advantage':float(incoming[best]-incoming[second]),'suffix_likelihood_log_ranges':[[float(min(x)),float(max(x))] for x in like.T],'suffix_correction_log_ranges':[[float(min(x)),float(max(x))] for x in correction.T],'coordinates':coordinates}
     records.append(record)
     with (out/'suffix-weight-components.csv').open('w') as f:
      w=csv.writer(f);w.writerow(['index','incoming_log_weight','suffix_log_likelihood','suffix_log_correction','ancestor_log_weight','ancestor_mass','prefix_latitude_deg','terminal_latitude_deg'])
      for i in range(len(raw)):w.writerow([i,incoming[i],sum(like[i]),sum(correction[i]),full[i],p[i],*coordinates[i]])
    result={'status':'passed','contacts_checked':contacts_checked,'maximum_likelihood_error':maximum_ll_error,'maximum_ancestor_log_weight_error':maximum_weight_error,'records':records,'checker_sha256':sha(__file__),'scope':__doc__+' Counterfactual component-only normalizations diagnose numerical concentration; they are not altered scientific posteriors.'}
    (ROOT/'suffix-verification.json').write_text(json.dumps(result,indent=2)+'\n')
    print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in result.items() if k"'!='"'records'}))
    for r in records:print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in r.items() if k"'!='"'coordinates'}))
    PY
      exit code 0
